This intake is in progress: new titles remain in [the pending queue](../data/shelves_261025/pending.json). No completeness claim for their assessment.

### New: The Revenge of Geography: What the Map Tells Us About Coming Conflicts and the Battle Against Fate — Robert D. Kaplan

Mandatory Karpathy review and matching Subcritical Appraisal occurrence counted once each. Explanatory nonfiction passes hard gates. Kirkus and Harm de Blij praise its breadth and geographical insight; these defenses preserve counterfactual value. They do not remove the substantial critique of its recurring causal narrative. No single historical period: cases support contemporary geopolitical claims. Global scope prevents T11; geopolitics dominates T12. Not selected.

Status: vetoed. Verdict: Vetoed; counterfactual score; confidence moderate. Veto V9; confidence high. Geographer Ron Johnston directly challenges the book's central reduction of geopolitical explanation to configuration, topography and distance, and its selective reliance on older thinkers while omitting contemporary political geography. The causal framework is the target, not a dispute about individual forecasts. The Population and Development Review critique independently finds the central claims unsupported by the descriptive evidence. Counterfactual score only.

### New: Rationality: From AI to Zombies — Eliezer Yudkowsky

Mandatory Karpathy coverage, with Subcritical and McAteer occurrences merged once each. Published ebook compilation, with component print volumes verified by MIRI; not an unpublished blog reference. General epistemology and cognitive-bias explanation, not a specialist technical curriculum. Religious criticism and personal motivation are parts rather than the dominant purpose, so V7/V8 do not automatically apply. Miller praises useful reasoning material and discloses support for MIRI; Goertzel challenges the AI-risk framework. That disagreement does not by itself establish a veto on this broad compilation's central reasoning content. Do not treat contested quantum interpretation or AI claims as settled. Philosophy central, T12=-10. Not selected: very long, unevenly edited and disputed specialist applications.

Status: recommended. Verdict: Poor fit; confidence moderate.
